PID regional buses · Praha-východ (okres Praha-východ) · Monday 2 Nov 2026

PID Praha-východ

Every PID bus line of okres Praha-východ (licences 28xxxx) and the Prague and other lines whose outer end lies here, set as Simutrans lines with the owner's regional method: each line read over the whole day at its core points, a repeating interval, destination and shift variants on their own longer intervals (at most 480 min), timetables at the terminals worked back from the knots, every afternoon stop on a line, buses sized from the pattern. Okres Praha-východ: the regional lines around Brandýs nad Labem, Úvaly, Říčany and Strančice, the Prague lines whose outer end is in the okres (Černý Most, Kobylisy, Letňany, Opatov, Budějovická) and the Říčany town lines (MHD Říčany). Every slot is a real departure minute read over the whole day at the core points; where a line has several ends each end is its own Simutrans line, interleaved as the timetable interleaves them. Outstation ends (the buses sleep there and the afternoon sends more out of the town than come back) are timed at the inbound rate, the extra departures listed: 335 at Kamenice, 371 at Klecany, 669 in the Sudovo Hlavno villages. One cross-line circuit: 344 with 343's Horoušany – Nehvizdy trips, which every 344 bus there runs (the 0-minute chains, 9 of 9), 3 buses instead of 4. 482 is modelled at its afternoon peak, every 30 from 14:15 to 16:52 (window 14:30–16:30, 6 buses); hourly :39 / :45 at 16:00 would be 4 buses with the six extra trips listed. Through-running seen in the timetable, for the 2 Nov live map: 354 with 662 at Mochov (12 of 17), 335 with 444 at Kamenice (10 of 21) and its Želivec shorts with 339, 482's extra trips with 353 and 655 at Brandýs, nádraží, 653 with 490 at Ondřejov. Real 0-minute turns (344 at Horoušany, 411 at Brnky, 484 at Úvaly, 685 at Černé Voděrady) are typed 1 min early by the loop rule and rely on the entry's automatic Leave up to. More 0–5 minute chains for the live map: at Brandýs, autobusové stanoviště 669 departures follow a 379 arrival (12 of 19) or a 367 arrival (10 of 19) and 666 departures a 367 (8 of 14) or 379 (6 of 14) arrival: the buses from Prague run on into the villages, which is also why 669 is an outstation line; 321 → 657 at Mratín (2 of 2, the morning Mratín shorts); at Úvaly, železniční stanice 655 → 823 (6 of 15) and 391 → 484 / 823 (7 of 11); 686 → 435 at Říčany, Wolkerova (5 of 13, another sheet). For the live map's afternoon plates (not yet in the reading files when this was designed): the outstation departures left off were chosen by the waits (the 290 rule): 335 (Budějovická 15:31, 16:33), 669 (Brandýs 16:35), 371 Astrapark (Kobylisy 15:53, 16:08, 16:23, 16:53). Pooling options seen in the timetable, not applied: 461 at Strančice, 328 with 328 Čestlice at Opatov, 302 with 302 Vinořská at Letňany, 823 with 823 Škvorec at Úvaly (each about one bus fewer). Stops served here only by unmodelled school lines and all afternoon by a sibling sheet's line: Vyšehořovice (662, Kolínsko), Polerady, rozcestí (377, Mělnicko); Postřižín and Neratovice, Lobkovice, škola are those sheets' own stops.

How to read the line cards

Typing the timetable. Values go into the Scheduling tab of the terminal entry, with the calendar on: Departure every, Offset, Also at (at most 7). Offsets count from 01:00 and every terminal is set as a loop, so each value is (real departure − 61) mod interval; the real clock minute is under every value. A row with Every 0 has no timetable: the bus leaves on arrival.

Core points and knots. Each line was read over the whole operating day (5:00–23:00) at its core points: terminals, knots where variants split, shared hubs. A trip counts at the time it passes a core point, wherever it starts. Where a slot is defined at a knot, the terminal departure is the knot time minus the running time, and the knot gets no timetable.

Variants. Each destination, path or shift variant is its own Simutrans line on a longer interval (the save game's Ex4). Shift lines repeat every 480 (8 h), the longest interval allowed. Trips more than 3 min off every slot are off the pattern, in orange.

Buses needed. One bus followed round the line's own slots with the real running times and at least 3 min at each terminal. Real buses swap lines at the terminals, so per-line totals are higher than what the operators run; pooling lines is the owner's choice and is not done.

In the game. Each card compares the save's line (export of 30 Oct 2026) with the modelled stops. The save's buses are shown as a check; the sheet is built from the real timetable and the buses seen running, so a game line may need buses added or moved.

To check

Choices made here that you may want to flip, each with its alternative; then the Simutrans lines whose model window has a slot without a real departure, or a real departure more than 3 min off.

How this was built

Scope. PID GTFS downloaded Sun 1 Nov 2026 (feed 1-14 Nov). Service day Mon 2 Nov 2026 (the first live-map poll day), a normal school weekday (28 Oct was a holiday and 29-30 Oct autumn school holidays; regional lines run many school-day-only trips). The Středočeský kraj licenses each district's lines with its own prefix (28xxxx = okres Praha-východ); a line licensed by Prague or by another kraj is on the sheet of the okres of its outer end (the first stop, coming in from the far end, in a Středočeský municipality), or, with no Prague end, of the okres most of its stops lie in. 69 lines: 50 modelled here, 8 on the Prague bus sheet, 11 listed with the reason.

Method. pipeline/bus-schedule-method.md (v4): whole-day reading at core points, repeating interval, Ex4 variants, timing at terminals worked back from the knots, no daily lines (at most every 480), every afternoon stop on a line, buses from the pattern, every line checked by hand (method-check file in the pipeline folder).

Buses. PID live map (mapa.pid.cz getData.php), polled every minute 12:00-20:00 (every 3 min 5:00-12:00 and 20:00-23:00) on Mon 2 Nov 2026, and on Tue 3 Nov 2026 so far only 5:00-7:20 (this version, 3 Nov morning: the Tuesday trips fill only designed buses and missing types; the evening version adds the whole Tuesday), by scripts/01_live_poll.py; each bus once per line through getVehicleWindow.php (operator, type, course). A bus fills one Simutrans line it ran in that line's window, by matching, across all twelve oblast sheets; buses on the Prague bus sheet are not used here.

Sources: PID GTFS, PID live map, the save-game export of 30 Oct 2026 (engine session).
